 173: 
 174: RE_NCT = re.compile(r"\bNCT\d{8}\b", re.I)
 175: # a citation is a bracket holding only record ids; anything else stays text
 176: RE_CITE = re.compile(r"\[\s*(?:(?:NCT\d{8}|CHEMBL\d+|ENSG\d+|PMID:?\s*\d+|ORG:[A-Za-z0-9_.\-]+)"
 177:                      r"\s*[,;]?\s*)+\]", re.I)
 178: RE_MDLINK = re.compile(r"\[([^\]]*)\]\([^)]*\)")
 179: ROMAN = {"i": "1", "ii": "2", "iii": "3", "iv": "4"}
 180: RE_TOKEN = re.compile(r"\x00(\d+)\x00|\d+[a-z]+\b|\d+(?:,\d{3})*(?:\.\d+)?%?"
 181:                       r"|[a-z]+(?:_[a-z]+)*(?:'[a-z]+)?|n't|[,;:]")
 182: 
 183: 
 184: def _flat(s: str) -> str:
 185:     return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", " ", s.lower().replace("(s)", "s")).strip()
 186: 
 187: 
 188: def _squash(s: str) -> str:
 189:     return re.sub(r"[\s_\-]+", "", s.lower())
 190: 
 191: 
 192: def _has(label: str, low: str) -> bool:
 193:     """Whole-word containment: "ALK" must not match "alkylating"."""
 194:     return re.search(rf"(?<![a-z0-9]){re.escape(label.lower())}(?![a-z0-9])", low) is not None
 195: 
 196: 
 197: def _non_english(s: str) -> bool:
 198:     return any(ord(c) > 127 and unicodedata.category(c).startswith("L") for c in s)
 199: 
 200: 
 201: # -- units -------------------------------------------------------------------- #
 202: def _sentences(body: str) -> list[str]:
 203:     out, depth, start = [], 0, 0
 204:     for i, ch in enumerate(body):
 205:         depth += ch == "("
 206:         depth -= ch == ")"
 207:         end = (ch in ".!?" and (i + 1 == len(body) or body[i + 1] == " ")
 208:                and not re.search(r"\b(?:vs|e\.g|i\.e|approx|no)\.?$", body[start:i], re.I))
 209:         if end or (ch == ";" and depth <= 0):
 210:             out.append(body[start:i + 1].strip())
 211:             start = i + 1
 212:     out.append(body[start:].strip())
 213:     return out
 214: 
 215: 
 216: def _strip_citations(text: str, known: set[str]) -> str:
 217:     """Drop "[NCT…, ORG:…]" only when every id in it is a record in the evidence;
 218:     anything else in brackets is read as text."""
 219:     def drop(m):
 220:         ids = [x.strip().lower() for x in re.split(r"[,;\s]+", m.group(0).strip("[] "))
 221:                if x.strip()]
 222:         return " " if ids and all(i in known for i in ids) else m.group(0)
 223:     return RE_CITE.sub(drop, text)
 224: 
 225: 
 226: def _units(text: str) -> list[dict]:
 227:     """Sentences. A list item under a heading carries the heading with it."""
 228:     text = RE_MDLINK.sub(r"\1", text)
 229:     out: list[dict] = []
 230:     heading, used = None, False
 231: 
 232:     def close_heading():
 233:         if heading is not None and not used:      # a heading with no items is prose
 234:             out.extend({"text": s} for s in _sentences(heading))
 235: 
 236:     for raw in text.splitlines() + [""]:
 237:         line = raw.strip()
 238:         bullet = re.match(r"^(?:[-*•]|\d+[.)])\s+", line)
 239:         body = line[bullet.end():] if bullet else line
 240:         if bullet and heading is not None:
 241:             out.append({"text": body, "heading": heading})
 242:             used = True
 243:             continue
 244:         close_heading()
 245:         heading, used = None, False
 246:         if not line:
 247:             continue
 248:         if body.endswith(":") and not bullet:
 249:             heading = body[:-1]
 250:             continue
 251:         out.extend({"text": s} for s in _sentences(body))
 252:     return [u for u in out if re.search(r"[^\W\d_]", u["text"] + u.get("heading", ""))]
 253: 
 254: 
 255: def _join(item: str, heading: str) -> str:
 256:     if not item:
 257:         return heading
 258:     # the slot is a determiner and its plain noun ("these NSCLC drugs"); an adjective
 259:     # ("the following safe drugs") is a claim and stays in the sentence
 260:     slot = re.search(r"\b(?:these|the following|following)(?:\s+(?:NSCLC\s+)?(?:drugs|agents|trials|"
 261:                      r"companies|sponsors|targets|molecules|compounds|organisations|"
 262:                      r"organizations|items|ones|records|entries)\b)?", heading, re.I)
 263:     if slot:
 264:         return heading[:slot.start()] + item + heading[slot.end():]
 265:     return f"{item} — {heading}"
 266: 
 267: 
 268: # -- the check ---------------------------------------------------------------- #
 269: class _State:
 270:     def __init__(self, question_entities):
 271:         self.by_type: dict[str, dict] = {}
 272:         self.subject: dict | None = None
 273:         for e in question_entities or []:
 274:             if e.get("type") in ("Drug", "Target", "Company"):
 275:                 self.by_type.setdefault(e["type"], e)
 276:                 self.subject = self.subject or e
 277: 
 278:         self.group: list[dict] = [self.subject] if self.subject else []
 279:         self.last: list[dict] = list(self.group)
 280: 
 281:     def note(self, ents, group=None):
 282:         real = [e for e in ents if e and e.get("type") in ("Drug", "Target", "Company", "Trial")]
 283:         if real:
 284:             self.subject = real[0]
 285:             self.group = [e for e in (group or []) if e in real] or [real[0]]
 286:             self.last = real
 287:             for e in real:
 288:                 self.by_type[e["type"]] = e
 289: 
 290:     def restore(self, members, last):
 291:         """After a clause checked once per member of a group, the group still stands."""
 292:         self.subject, self.group, self.last = members[0], list(members), list(last)
 293: 
 294: 
 295: def check_claims(text: str, evidence: list, linker, question_entities=None) -> list[dict]:
 296:     """Return one finding per unsupported claim. `linker(text)` -> entities.
 297: 
 298:     `question_entities` seed what "this drug" / "this target" refer to."""
 299:     statements = [(e.relation, (e.statement or "").lower()) for e in evidence]
 300:     flat_statements = {_flat(s) for _, s in statements}
 301:     findings: list[dict] = []
 302:     st = _State(question_entities)
 303: 
 304:     known = set()
 305:     for e in evidence:
 306:         for part in [e.source_record or "", e.edge_id or "", e.statement or ""]:
 307:             known |= {x.strip().lower() for x in re.split(r"[,;\s|]+", part) if x.strip()}
 308:     text = _strip_citations(text, known)
 309:     for u in _units(text):
 310:         item, heading = u["text"], u.get("heading")
 311:         unit = _join(item.rstrip("."), heading) if heading else item
 312: 
 313:         def flag(kind, value, why, unit=unit):
 314:             findings.append({"kind": kind, "value": value, "sentence": unit, "why": why})
 315: 
 316:         if _non_english(unit):
 317:             flag("unreadable", unit[:40], "text in a language the check cannot read is "
 318:                                           "not verified")
 319:             continue
 320:         if _flat(item.rstrip(".")) in flat_statements:
 321:             # a verbatim evidence line says exactly what the evidence says; it still
 322:             # sets the subject for what follows, and its heading is still read
 323:             item_ents = ([{"type": "Trial", "label": m.upper()} for m in RE_NCT.findall(item)]
 324:                          + [e for e in linker(item)
 325:                             if e.get("type") in ("Drug", "Target", "Company")])
 326:             if heading:
 327:                 # the item's own subject: its words up to the first verb
 328:                 lead = re.split(r"\b(?:is|are|was|were|has|have|acts?|evaluates?|sponsors?)\b",
 329:                                 re.sub(r"\([^)]*\)", " ", item), maxsplit=1)[0].strip()
 330:                 # an unlinkable name ("Tianjin Medical University ...") fills nothing;
 331:                 # the item itself is already verified
 332:                 linked = RE_NCT.search(lead) or any(
 333:                     e.get("type") in ("Drug", "Target", "Company") for e in linker(lead))
 334:                 fill = lead if linked else ""
 335:                 _check_unit(_join(fill, heading), statements, linker, st, flag,
 336:                             joined=bool(fill))
 337:             st.note(item_ents)
 338:             continue
 339:         _check_unit(unit, statements, linker, st, flag)
 340:     return findings
 341: 
 342: 
 343: def _check_unit(unit, statements, linker, st, flag, lenient=False, joined=False):
 344:     toks, ents = _tokenise(unit, linker)
 345:     for clause in _clauses(toks):
 346:         # pronouns take the subject as it stands at this clause, not at the unit start;
 347:         # one after a relation verb is that relation's object ("targets EGFR and
 348:         # inhibits it"); a plural one stands for every member of the subject group
 349:         plural = None
 350:         for k, t in enumerate(clause):
 351:             if t[0] != "ENT" or ents[t[1]]["type"] != "Pronoun":
 352:                 continue
 353:             p = ents[t[1]]
 354:             want = p["want"]
 355:             back = [x for x in clause[max(0, k - 2):k] if x[0] == "W"]
 356:             if want is None and back and back[-1][1] in set(RELATION) | {"on", "of"}:
 357:                 verb = next((x[1] for x in reversed(back) if x[1] in RELATION), None)
 358:                 if verb:
 359:                     want = ROLES[RELATION[verb][0]][1]
 360:             if want is None:
 361:                 # "they" (or an "it" that could be either) after a clause naming several
 362:                 # entities of one kind stands for all of them: the subject group, or
 363:                 # else the largest set of one kind ("ALK is targeted by A and B")
 364:                 kinds_last: dict[str, list] = {}
 365:                 for e in st.last:
 366:                     kinds_last.setdefault(e.get("type"), []).append(e)
 367:                 biggest = max(kinds_last.values(), key=len, default=[])
 368:                 members = (list(st.group) if len(st.group) > 1 else
 369:                            biggest if p["label"] in PLURAL and len(biggest) > 1 else [])
 370:             else:
 371:                 members = ([e for e in st.last if e.get("type") == want]
 372:                            if p["label"] in PLURAL else [])
 373:             if len(members) > 1:
 374:                 plural = (t[1], members)
 375:                 ents[t[1]] = dict(members[0])
 376:                 continue
 377:             ante = st.by_type.get(want) if want else st.subject
 378:             ents[t[1]] = (dict(ante, _pron=True) if ante
 379:                           else {"type": "Missing", "label": p["label"]})
 380:         pred = next((i for i, t in enumerate(clause)
 381:                      if t[0] == "W" and t[1] in PREDICATE), len(clause))
 382:         elided = pred < len(clause) and not any(t[0] == "ENT" for t in clause[:pred])
 383:         if plural:
 384:             idx, members = plural
 385:             for m in members:                 # "they are approved": each of them
 386:                 ents[idx] = dict(m)
 387:                 _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)
 388:             if idx in [t[1] for t in clause[:pred] if t[0] == "ENT"]:
 389:                 st.restore(members, members)  # "they" stays the group for what follows
 390:             else:
 391:                 names = {m["label"] for m in members}
 392:                 st.last = [e for e in st.last if e.get("label") not in names] + members
 393:         elif elided and len(st.group) > 1 and not lenient:
 394:             members, last = list(st.group), list(st.last)
 395:             for m in members:                 # "LORLATINIB and PLB1003 ... and are approved"
 396:                 st.subject = m
 397:                 _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)
 398:             st.restore(members, last)
 399:         else:
 400:             _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)
 401: 
 402: 
 403: def _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient=False, joined=False):
 404:     if True:
 405:         c_ents = [ents[t[1]] for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"]
 406:         words = [t[1] for t in clause if t[0] == "W"]
 407:         has_num = any(t[0] == "N" for t in clause)
 408: 
 409:         refused = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
 410:         for e in refused:
 411:             flag("distinct_product", e["label"], "names a distinct product (biosimilar, "
 412:                  "other formulation, tracer) the graph does not hold")
 413:         c_ents = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] != "Refused"]
 414: 
 415:         gone = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] == "Missing"]
 416:         if len(gone) == len(c_ents) and not has_num and all(
 417:                 w in META_OK or w.endswith("n't") or w in FUNCTION
 418:                 or (w in KIND_WORDS and META_ANCHOR & set(words)) for w in words) and (
 419:                 not any(w in KIND_WORDS or w in FUNCTION for w in words if w not in META_OK)
 420:                 or META_ANCHOR & set(words)):
 421:             return                            # "this graph cannot answer that"
 422:         if gone:
 423:             flag("no_antecedent", gone[0]["label"],
 424:                  "a pronoun with nothing before it to refer to")
 425:             return
 426: 
 427:         pred = next((i for i, t in enumerate(clause)
 428:                      if t[0] == "W" and t[1] in PREDICATE), len(clause))
 429:         implicit = None
 430:         if pred < len(clause) and not lenient and not any(
 431:                 t[0] == "ENT" and ents[t[1]]["type"] != "Refused" for t in clause[:pred]):
 432:             implicit = st.subject             # "... and is phase 3"
 433:         subjects = c_ents + ([implicit] if implicit and implicit not in c_ents else [])
 434:         if not subjects:
 435:             content = [w for w in words if w not in FUNCTION and w not in BE]
 436:             if content or has_num:
 437:                 flag("no_subject", " ".join(content[:6]) or "figure",
 438:                      "this clause names no entity the evidence can be checked against")
 439:             return
 440:         labels = [e["label"].lower() for e in subjects]
 441:         support = [(r, s) for r, s in statements if all(_has(l, s) for l in labels)]
 442:         covered = _check_relations(clause, ents, implicit, statements, flag, lenient)
 443:         left_e = {ents[t[1]]["label"].lower() for t in clause[:pred] if t[0] == "ENT"} or (
 444:             {implicit["label"].lower()} if implicit else set())
 445:         right_e = {ents[t[1]]["label"].lower() for t in clause[pred:] if t[0] == "ENT"}
 446:         if covered is None and pred < len(clause) and left_e and right_e and (
 447:                 left_e != right_e or len(left_e) > 1):
 448:             # "PLB1003 is LORLATINIB": two entities joined by a verb that is no relation
 449:             flag("identity", " = ".join(e["label"] for e in c_ents),
 450:                  "two different entities joined by a verb the graph has no edge for")
 451:         # under a joined heading the first entity is the copied line's subject and the
 452:         # dash is ours; the heading's own named entities still may not pair silently
 453:         own = ([ents[t[1]] for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"][1:] if joined else c_ents)
 454:         own = [e for e in own if not e.get("_pron")]
 455:         if covered is None and pred == len(clause) and not lenient and (
 456:                 not joined or own):
 457:             kinds = {e["type"] for e in c_ents}
 458:             first = next((t for t in clause if t[0] != "P"), None)
 459:             if len(kinds) > 1 or (first and first[0] == "W" and first[1] in
 460:                                   ("on", "of", "to", "in", "with", "for", "against", "by")):
 461:                 flag("fragment", " ".join(e["label"] for e in c_ents) or first[1],
 462:                      "a clause with no verb: the relation it implies cannot be read")
 463:         if covered is not None and not lenient:
 464:             for t in clause:
 465:                 if t[0] == "ENT" and t[1] not in covered and ents[t[1]]["type"] != "Refused":
 466:                     flag("unrelated_entity", ents[t[1]]["label"],
 467:                          "named in a clause that states a relation, but not part of any "
 468:                          "relation that was checked")
 469:         _check_attributes(clause, ents, subjects, support, statements, flag)
 470:         group = [ents[t[1]] for t in clause[:pred] if t[0] == "ENT"]
 471:         st.note(c_ents, group)
 472: 
 473: 
 474: def _tokenise(unit, linker):
 475:     """Entities and pronouns become placeholders."""
 476:     s = unit
 477:     for pat in SCOPE_PHRASES:
 478:         s = re.sub(pat, " nsclc ", s, flags=re.I)
 479:     s = s.replace("(s)", "s").replace("’", "'")
 480:     s = re.sub(r"[()\[\]{}\"*—–]", " ", s)
 481:     ents: list[dict] = []
 482:     spans = []
 483:     for m in RE_NCT.finditer(s):
 484:         spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), {"type": "Trial", "label": m.group(0).upper()}))
 485: 
 486:     def blanked():
 487:         b = list(s)
 488:         for a, z, _ in spans:
 489:             b[a:z] = " " * (z - a)
 490:         # the linker strips only " ?.," from tokens, so "EGFR;" or "ALK:" would not link
 491:         return re.sub(r"[;:!]", " ", "".join(b))
 492: 
 493:     # the linker reports each entity once, so "Osimertinib (Tagrisso)" needs a second look
 494:     for _ in range(8):
 495:         new = 0
 496:         for e in linker(blanked()):
 497:             if e.get("type") == "Disease":
 498:                 continue
 499:             words = [re.escape(w) for w in (e.get("matched_text") or "").split()]
 500:             if not words:
 501:                 continue
 502:             pat = r"(?<![A-Za-z0-9])" + r"[\W_]+".join(words) + r"(?![A-Za-z0-9])"
 503:             for m in re.finditer(pat, s, re.I):
 504:                 if not any(a < m.end() and m.start() < b for a, b, _ in spans):
 505:                     spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), e))
 506:                     new += 1
 507:         if not new:
 508:             break
 509:     low = s.lower()
 510:     for phrase, typ in sorted(PRONOUN.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):
 511:         for m in re.finditer(rf"\b{re.escape(phrase)}\b", low):
 512:             if any(a < m.end() and m.start() < b for a, b, _ in spans):
 513:                 continue
 514:             after = low[m.end():]
 515:             if re.match(r"\s+of\b", after) or any(
 516:                     a >= m.end() and not s[m.end():a].strip(" ,") for a, _, _ in spans):
 517:                 continue                 # "the target of X", "the drug osimertinib"
 518:             spans.append((m.start(), m.end(), {"type": "Pronoun", "label": phrase,
 519:                                                "want": typ}))
 520:     spans.sort()
 521:     out, last = [], 0
 522:     for a, b, e in spans:
 523:         out.append(s[last:a])
 524:         out.append(f" \x00{len(ents)}\x00 ")
 525:         ents.append(e)
 526:         last = b
 527:     out.append(s[last:])
 528:     toks = []
 529:     for m in RE_TOKEN.finditer("".join(out).lower()):
 530:         if m.group(1) is not None:
 531:             toks.append(("ENT", int(m.group(1))))
 532:         elif m.group(0) in ",;:":
 533:             toks.append(("P", m.group(0)))
 534:         elif m.group(0)[0].isdigit() and not re.fullmatch(r"\d+[a-z]+", m.group(0)):
 535:             toks.append(("N", m.group(0).replace(",", "")))
 536:         else:
 537:             toks.append(("W", m.group(0)))
 538:     return toks, ents
 539: 
 540: 
 541: def _clauses(toks):
 542:     """Split at ; and at and/but/while/whereas when both sides have a predicate."""
 543:     joins = ("and", "but", "while", "whereas")
 544:     cuts = [-1]
 545:     for i, t in enumerate(toks):
 546:         if t == ("P", ";"):
 547:             cuts.append(i)
 548:         elif t[0] == "W" and t[1] in joins:
 549:             left = toks[cuts[-1] + 1:i]
 550:             nxt = next((j for j in range(i + 1, len(toks))
 551:                         if (toks[j][0] == "W" and toks[j][1] in joins)
 552:                         or toks[j] == ("P", ";")), len(toks))
 553:             right = toks[i + 1:nxt]
 554:             if any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in PREDICATE for x in left) and \
 555:                     any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in PREDICATE for x in right):
 556:                 cuts.append(i)
 557:     cuts.append(len(toks))
 558:     return [toks[a + 1:b] for a, b in zip(cuts, cuts[1:]) if toks[a + 1:b]]
 559: 
 560: 
 561: def _group(clause, k, step, skip=()):
 562:     """Positions of the entity run nearest to k in direction `step`."""
 563:     i = k + step
 564:     while 0 <= i < len(clause) and (clause[i][0] != "ENT" or i in skip):
 565:         i += step
 566:     grp = []
 567:     while 0 <= i < len(clause):
 568:         if clause[i][0] == "ENT" and i not in skip:
 569:             grp.append(i)
 570:             i += step
 571:         elif clause[i] in GROUP_GLUE:
 572:             i += step
 573:         elif step > 0 and grp and clause[i - 1] == ("W", "and"):
 574:             # an elided repeat: "... of 14 trials of A and 10 trials of B" -> A, B
 575:             j = i
 576:             while j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] in ("N", "W") and not (
 577:                     clause[j][0] == "W" and clause[j][1] in PREDICATE):
 578:                 j += 1
 579:             if j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] == "ENT" and j not in skip:
 580:                 i = j
 581:                 continue
 582:             break
 583:         else:
 584:             break
 585:     return grp
 586: 
 587: 
 588: def _is_passive(clause, k):
 589:     """A participle right after a form of "be" (adverbs allowed), or before "by"."""
 590:     if k + 1 < len(clause) and clause[k + 1] == ("W", "by"):
 591:         return True
 592:     i = k - 1
 593:     while i >= 0 and clause[i][0] == "W" and clause[i][1] in PASSIVE_GAP:
 594:         i -= 1
 595:     return i >= 0 and clause[i][0] == "W" and clause[i][1] in BE
 596: 
 597: 
 598: def _check_relations(clause, ents, implicit, statements, flag, lenient):
 599:     """Check every relation in the clause; return the clause positions it covered,
 600:     or None when the clause states no relation."""
 601:     covered: set[int] = set()
 602:     any_rel = False
 603:     for k, t in enumerate(clause):
 604:         if t[0] == "W" and t[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS and k + 1 < len(clause) and \
 605:                 clause[k + 1] == ("W", "of"):
 606:             # "<trial> is a phase 1 trial of <drug>": the trial evaluates the drug;
 607:             # "<company> sponsors 14 trials of <drug>": the sponsorship carries it
 608:             drugs = _group(clause, k, +1)
 609:             trials = [i for i in _group(clause, k, -1)
 610:                       if ents[clause[i][1]]["type"] == "Trial"]
 611:             if drugs and not trials:
 612:                 j = max(drugs) + 1
 613:                 if j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] == "W" and clause[j][1] in (
 614:                         "include", "includes", "including", "are", "were"):
 615:                     trials = _group(clause, j, +1)
 616:                     bad = [i for i in trials if ents[clause[i][1]]["type"] != "Trial"]
 617:                     if bad:
 618:                         trials = [i for i in trials if i not in bad]
 619:             if drugs and trials:
 620:                 any_rel = True
 621:                 covered |= {clause[i][1] for i in drugs + trials}
 622:                 for x in trials:
 623:                     for y in drugs:
 624:                         X, Y = ents[clause[x][1]], ents[clause[y][1]]
 625:                         if not any(r == "EVALUATES" and _has(X["label"], s)
 626:                                    and _has(Y["label"], s) for r, s in statements):
 627:                             flag("relation", f"{X['label']} — trial of — {Y['label']}",
 628:                                  "no retrieved edge links these two entities this way")
 629:             continue
 630:         if t[0] != "W" or t[1] not in RELATION:
 631:             continue
 632:         if t[1] == "sponsor" and k and clause[k - 1] == ("W", "lead"):
 633:             continue                       # "lead sponsor": one noun, read at "lead"
 634:         any_rel = True
 635:         rel, action, voice = RELATION[t[1]]
 636:         subj_t, obj_t = ROLES[rel]
 637:         prev = clause[k - 1] if k else None
 638:         nxt = clause[k + 1] if k + 1 < len(clause) else None
 639: 
 640:         if t[1] in AGENT_NOUNS:
 641:             # "X is an inhibitor of Y", "X is a Y inhibitor": X acts, Y is acted on
 642:             before = clause[k - 2] if k >= 2 else None
 643:             if prev and prev[0] == "ENT" and (
 644:                     (before and before[0] == "W" and before[1] in ARTICLES)
 645:                     or ents[prev[1]]["type"] == obj_t):
 646:                 obj, subj = [k - 1], _group(clause, k - 1, -1)
 647:             else:
 648:                 obj = _group(clause, k, +1) if nxt == ("W", "of") else []
 649:                 subj = _group(clause, k, -1)
 650:             noun = True
 651:         elif t[1] in ("target", "targets") and nxt == ("W", "of"):
 652:             # "EGFR is the target of osimertinib" / "the target of osimertinib is EGFR"
 653:             subj = _group(clause, k, +1)
 654:             obj = _group(clause, k, -1)
 655:             if not obj and subj:
 656:                 j = max(subj) + 1
 657:                 while j < len(clause) and clause[j] in GROUP_GLUE:
 658:                     j += 1
 659:                 if j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] == "W" and clause[j][1] in BE:
 660:                     obj = _group(clause, j, +1)
 661:             noun = True
 662:         else:
 663:             subj, obj = _group(clause, k, -1), _group(clause, k, +1)
 664:             if voice == "passive" and _is_passive(clause, k):
 665:                 if obj:
 666:                     between = {x[1] for x in clause[k + 1:min(obj)] if x[0] == "W"}
 667:                     allowed = {"by"} | ({"in", "at"} if rel == "EVALUATES" else set())
 668:                     if not between & allowed:
 669:                         flag("relation_direction", f"{t[1]} without 'by'",
 670:                              "a passive verb whose agent is not introduced by 'by'; the "
 671:                              "sentence cannot be read in one direction")
 672:                         any_rel = True
 673:                         covered |= {clause[i][1] for i in subj + obj}
 674:                         continue
 675:                 subj, obj = obj, subj
 676:             noun = t[1] in ("sponsor", "sponsors") and (
 677:                 (prev and prev[0] == "W" and (prev[1] in ARTICLES or prev[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS))
 678:                 or nxt == ("W", "of") or nxt is None)
 679:         left = [ents[clause[i][1]] for i in subj]
 680:         right = [ents[clause[i][1]] for i in obj]
 681:         if not left and implicit:
 682:             left = [implicit]
 683:         if noun and left and not right:
 684:             # "AstraZeneca is a trial sponsor" (of the drug named in the clause)
 685:             pool = [i for i, x in enumerate(clause) if x[0] == "ENT" and i not in subj
 686:                     and ents[x[1]]["type"] == obj_t]
 687:             obj, right = pool, [ents[clause[i][1]] for i in pool]
 688:         here = {clause[i][1] for i in subj + obj}
 689: 
 690:         if rel == "EVALUATES" and not any(e["type"] == "Trial" for e in left + right):
 691:             # no named trial: the subject must be the word "trials" ("trials
 692:             # evaluating X") or the drug the grammatical object ("X is tested in")
 693:             passive = voice == "passive" and _is_passive(clause, k)
 694:             trial_subject = any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS
 695:                                 for x in clause[max(0, k - 3):k])
 696:             if not (passive or trial_subject):
 697:                 flag("relation_direction", f"{'/'.join(e['label'] for e in left)} {t[1]}",
 698:                      "trials evaluate drugs; this sentence has something else evaluating")
 699:                 continue
 700:             drugs = right
 701:             # with "trials" as the subject, the entity before it belongs to another relation
 702:             covered |= ({clause[i][1] for i in obj} if trial_subject and not passive
 703:                         else here)
 704:             # "trials evaluating X include NCT1 and NCT2": the listed trials evaluate X
 705:             if trial_subject and obj:
 706:                 j = max(obj) + 1
 707:                 if j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] == "W" and clause[j][1] in (
 708:                         "include", "includes", "including", "are", "were"):
 709:                     listed = _group(clause, j, +1)
 710:                     for x in listed:
 711:                         X = ents[clause[x][1]]
 712:                         covered.add(clause[x][1])
 713:                         for d in drugs:
 714:                             if X["type"] != "Trial" or not any(
 715:                                     r == "EVALUATES" and _has(X["label"], s)
 716:                                     and _has(d["label"], s) for r, s in statements):
 717:                                 flag("relation", f"{X['label']} — evaluates — {d['label']}",
 718:                                      "no retrieved edge links these two entities this way")
 719:             if not drugs or any(e["type"] != "Drug" for e in drugs):
 720:                 if not lenient:
 721:                     flag("relation_incomplete", t[1], "an evaluation relation whose "
 722:                                                       "object is not a drug")
 723:                 continue
 724:             for d in drugs:
 725:                 if not any(r in ("EVALUATES", "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF") and _has(d["label"], s)
 726:                            for r, s in statements):
 727:                     flag("relation", f"{d['label']} evaluated in trials",
 728:                          "no retrieved trial evaluates this drug")
 729:             continue
 730:         covered |= here
 731:         if not left or not right:
 732:             if not lenient:
 733:                 flag("relation_incomplete", t[1], "a relation whose two sides could not "
 734:                                                   "both be identified in the sentence")
 735:             continue
 736:         if any(e["type"] != subj_t for e in left) or any(e["type"] != obj_t for e in right):
 737:             flag("relation_direction",
 738:                  f"{'/'.join(e['label'] for e in left)} {t[1]} "
 739:                  f"{'/'.join(e['label'] for e in right)}",
 740:                  f"this relation runs from a {subj_t.lower()} to a {obj_t.lower()}; "
 741:                  "the sentence has it the other way or between the wrong kinds")
 742:             continue
 743:         for x in left:
 744:             for y in right:
 745:                 hits = [s for r, s in statements if r == rel and _has(x["label"], s)
 746:                         and _has(y["label"], s)]
 747:                 if not hits:
 748:                     flag("relation", f"{x['label']} — {t[1]} — {y['label']}",
 749:                          "no retrieved edge links these two entities this way")
 750:                 elif action and not any(re.search(rf"\b{a}\b", s)
 751:                                         for s in hits for a in ACTION_WORDS[action]):
 752:                     flag("action", f"{x['label']} {t[1]} {y['label']}",
 753:                          "the evidence gives a different or no action type")
 754:     return covered if any_rel else None
 755: 
 756: 
 757: def _owner(clause, ents, i, prefer, kinds=("Drug", "Target", "Company", "Trial")):
 758:     """The entity a value at position i belongs to: nearest in `prefer` direction first,
 759:     among the kinds of entity that can carry that value."""
 760:     order = [range(i + 1, len(clause)), range(i - 1, -1, -1)]
 761:     if prefer == "before":
 762:         order.reverse()
 763:     for rng in order:
 764:         for j in rng:
 765:             if clause[j][0] == "ENT" and ents[clause[j][1]]["type"] in kinds:
 766:                 return ents[clause[j][1]]
 767:     return None
 768: 
 769: 
 770: def _check_attributes(clause, ents, subjects, support, statements, flag):
 771:     subject = subjects[0]
 772:     present = {e["type"] for e in subjects} | {
 773:         ents[t[1]]["type"] for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"}
 774:     has_company = "Company" in present
 775:     companies = [e for e in subjects if e["type"] == "Company"]
 776: 
 777:     def scoped(i, prefer, count=False, kinds=("Drug", "Target", "Company", "Trial")):
 778:         """Evidence naming the clause subject, any company the clause is about (said or
 779:         carried over) and the value's own entity. A count of trials in a sponsorship
 780:         statement belongs to the sponsor-drug pair, so it does not count for a clause
 781:         that names no company."""
 782:         own = _owner(clause, ents, i, prefer, kinds) or subject
 783:         # a trial's phase is its own; any other value is about the clause's subject too
 784:         names = ({own["label"].lower()} if own["type"] == "Trial" else
 785:                  {own["label"].lower(), subject["label"].lower()} | {
 786:                      c["label"].lower() for c in companies})
 787:         rows = [s for r, s in statements if all(_has(n, s) for n in names)
 788:                 and not (count and r == "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF" and not has_company)]
 789:         return own, rows
 790: 
 791:     consumed: set[int] = set()
 792:     sup_tokens: set[str] = set()
 793:     for _, s in support:
 794:         sup_tokens |= set(re.findall(r"[a-z]+(?:_[a-z]+)*", s))
 795:     sup_flat = " " + " ".join(_flat(s) for _, s in support) + " "
 796:     # whether the clause is about trials (and NSCLC trials) is read from the statements
 797:     # pairing its subject with any of its entities, so a list of drugs can share it
 798:     others = {e["label"].lower() for e in subjects[1:]} | {
 799:         ents[t[1]]["label"].lower() for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"}
 800:     others.discard(subject["label"].lower())
 801:     # trials and NSCLC are about the clause's subject, not any entity it mentions
 802:     wide = [(r, s) for r, s in statements if _has(subject["label"], s)]
 803:     counted = any(t[0] == "N" and any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS
 804:                                       for x in clause[k + 1:k + 4])
 805:                   for k, t in enumerate(clause))
 806:     # a stated count of trials is itself checked as a number; otherwise the evidence
 807:     # must show trials for these entities (a count of zero shows none)
 808:     about_trials = any(r in ("EVALUATES", "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF")
 809:                        or re.search(r"(?<![\d.])[1-9]\d*\s+(?:\S+\s+)?trial", s)
 810:                        or (counted and "trial" in s)
 811:                        for r, s in wide)
 812:     wide_tokens = {w for _, s in wide for w in re.findall(r"[a-z]+", s)}
 813: 
 814:     def trial_run(at):
 815:         """The trials a value at `at` is about: the run of trials (joined by and/or/
 816:         commas) just before it, or else the first run after it."""
 817:         before = [k for k, t in enumerate(clause[:at]) if t[0] == "ENT"
 818:                   and ents[t[1]]["type"] == "Trial"]
 819:         if before:
 820:             run = _group(clause, before[-1] + 1, -1)
 821:         else:
 822:             after = next((k for k in range(at + 1, len(clause)) if clause[k][0] == "ENT"
 823:                           and ents[clause[k][1]]["type"] == "Trial"), None)
 824:             run = _group(clause, after - 1, +1) if after is not None else []
 825:         return [ents[clause[k][1]]["label"] for k in run
 826:                 if ents[clause[k][1]]["type"] == "Trial"]
 827: 
 828:     i = 0
 829:     while i < len(clause):
 830:         t = clause[i]
 831:         if t == ("W", "phase"):
 832:             j = i + 1
 833:             nums = []
 834:             while j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] in ("N", "W") and len(nums) < 2:
 835:                 v = clause[j][1]
 836:                 if clause[j][0] == "N" or v in ROMAN or re.fullmatch(r"\d+[a-z]+", v):
 837:                     nums.append(ROMAN.get(v, v))
 838:                     j += 1
 839:                 elif v in ("or", "to"):
 840:                     j += 1
 841:                 else:
 842:                     break
 843:             if nums:
 844:                 consumed |= set(range(i, j))
 845:                 key = "phase" + "".join(nums)
 846:                 # only a trial or a drug has a phase; with a trial named, it is the trial's
 847:                 owner, rows = scoped(i, "before", kinds=(("Trial",) if "Trial" in present
 848:                                                          else ("Trial", "Drug")))
 849:                 run = trial_run(i) if "Trial" in present else []
 850:                 if len(run) > 1:
 851:                     # "NCT1 and NCT2 are phase 3": each of them
 852:                     def has(lab):
 853:                         own1 = " ".join(_squash(x) for _, x in statements if _has(lab, x))
 854:                         return re.search(rf"{key}(?![0-9a-z])", own1) or all(
 855:                             re.search(rf"phase{n}(?![0-9a-z])", own1) for n in nums)
 856:                     ok = all(has(lab) for lab in run)
 857:                     if not ok:
 858:                         flag("phase", "phase " + "/".join(nums), "not every trial named "
 859:                              "here has that phase in the evidence")
 860:                     i = j
 861:                     continue
 862:                 own = " ".join(_squash(s) for s in rows)
 863:                 if owner["type"] == "Trial":
 864:                     ok = re.search(rf"{key}(?![0-9a-z])", own) or all(
 865:                         re.search(rf"phase{n}(?![0-9a-z])", own) for n in nums)
 866:                 elif owner["type"] == "Drug":
 867:                     # a drug's phase is its maximum stage, not the phase of a trial of it
 868:                     ok = re.search(rf"stage{key}(?![0-9a-z])", own)
 869:                 else:
 870:                     ok = False
 871:                 if not ok:
 872:                     flag("phase", "phase " + "/".join(nums), "no evidence statement about "
 873:                          "these entities gives that phase")
 874:                 i = j
 875:                 continue
 876:         if t[0] == "N":
 877:             n = t[1]
 878:             ahead = [x[1] for x in clause[i + 1:i + 4] if x[0] == "W"]
 879:             behind = [x[1] for x in clause[max(0, i - 3):i] if x[0] == "W"]
 880:             counting = any(w in TRIAL_NOUNS for w in ahead) or (
 881:                 bool(behind) and behind[-1] in TRIAL_NOUNS)        # "trials: 13"
 882:             own = " ".join(_flat(s) for s in scoped(i, "after", counting)[1]).replace(",", "")
 883:             if counting:
 884:                 # a count of trials must be stated as a count of trials, not a phase
 885:                 ok = re.search(rf"(?<!phase )(?<![\d.]){re.escape(n)}\s+(?:[a-z]+\s+){{0,2}}"
 886:                                r"trial", own)
 887:             else:
 888:                 # a bare figure, not the digit of a phase ("PHASE_1" is no count of 1)
 889:                 ok = re.search(rf"(?<!phase )(?<![\d.]){re.escape(n)}(?![\d.])", own)
 890:             if not ok:
 891:                 flag("number", n, "no evidence statement about these entities gives "
 892:                                   "that figure")
 893:         if t[0] == "W" and re.fullmatch(r"\d+[a-z]+", t[1]):
 894:             flag("number", t[1], "a figure with a suffix the evidence does not use")
 895:         i += 1
 896: 
 897:     # a count shared by several drugs in one clause cannot be told apart: each drug
 898:     # needs its own "N trials of/evaluating <drug>"
 899:     drugs, seen_labels = [], set()
 900:     for k, t in enumerate(clause):          # an alias in brackets is the same drug
 901:         if t[0] == "ENT" and ents[t[1]]["type"] == "Drug" and \
 902:                 ents[t[1]]["label"] not in seen_labels:
 903:             drugs.append(k)
 904:             seen_labels.add(ents[t[1]]["label"])
 905:     counts = [k for k, t in enumerate(clause) if t[0] == "N" and (
 906:         any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS for x in clause[k + 1:k + 4])
 907:         or any(x[0] == "W" and x[1] in TRIAL_NOUNS for x in clause[max(0, k - 2):k]))]
 908:     if counts and len(drugs) > 1:
 909:         owned = set()
 910:         for k in counts:
 911:             j = k + 1
 912:             while j < len(clause) and j < k + 6 and clause[j][0] != "ENT":
 913:                 j += 1
 914:             if j < len(clause) and clause[j][0] == "ENT" and j in drugs:
 915:                 owned.add(j)
 916:         if set(drugs) - owned:
 917:             flag("count_ambiguous", " / ".join(ents[clause[k][1]]["label"] for k in drugs),
 918:                  "a trial count in a clause naming several drugs, without a count for "
 919:                  "each drug of its own")
 920: 
 921:     # status phrases are one claim, in the registry's spelling, and belong to the
 922:     # trial named before them (or, with one subject, to the clause's entities)
 923:     trial_pos = [k for k, t in enumerate(clause) if t[0] == "ENT"
 924:                  and ents[t[1]]["type"] == "Trial"]
 925:     wpos = [k for k, t in enumerate(clause) if t[0] == "W" and k not in consumed]
 926:     wseq = [clause[k][1] for k in wpos]
 927:     taken: set[int] = set()
 928:     for status in STATUS:                    # longest phrases first, as listed
 929:         parts = status.split()
 930:         for j in range(len(wseq) - len(parts) + 1):
 931:             if wseq[j:j + len(parts)] != parts or taken & set(range(j, j + len(parts))):
 932:                 continue
 933:             taken |= set(range(j, j + len(parts)))
 934:             at = wpos[j]
 935:             if trial_pos:
 936:                 labs = trial_run(at) or [ents[clause[trial_pos[0]][1]]["label"]]
 937:                 row_sets = [[x for _, x in statements if _has(lab, x)] for lab in labs]
 938:             else:
 939:                 row_sets = [[x for _, x in support]]
 940:             for rows in row_sets:          # with several trials, each of them
 941:                 own = " " + " ".join(_flat(x) for x in rows) + " "
 942:                 plain = own.replace("active not recruiting", "").replace(
 943:                     "not yet recruiting", "")
 944:                 if f" {status} " not in own or (status == "recruiting"
 945:                                                 and " recruiting " not in plain):
 946:                     flag("status", status, "no evidence statement about that entity gives "
 947:                                            "that status")
 948:                     break
 949:     words = [w for j, w in enumerate(wseq) if j not in taken]
 950: 
 951: 
 952:     toks_w = [t[1] for t in clause if t[0] in ("W", "N")]
 953:     for j, w in enumerate(toks_w):
 954:         if w == "nsclc":
 955:             nxt = toks_w[j + 1] if j + 1 < len(toks_w) else ""
 956:             prv = toks_w[j - 1] if j else ""
 957:             # the disease may only qualify trials the evidence itself calls NSCLC trials:
 958:             # "NSCLC trials", or "in NSCLC" in a clause that is about trials, never a
 959:             # drug's approval or use ("approved in NSCLC")
 960:             trial_clause = any(x in TRIAL_NOUNS or (x in RELATION and RELATION[x][0] in (
 961:                 "EVALUATES", "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF")) for x in toks_w)
 962:             approval = any(x in ("approved", "approval", "approve", "approves",
 963:                                  "indicated", "indication", "licensed") for x in toks_w)
 964:             if approval or not ((nxt in TRIAL_NOUNS or (prv == "in" and trial_clause))
 965:                                 and "nsclc" in wide_tokens and about_trials):
 966:                 flag("indication", "lung cancer", "a claim about the disease itself (what a "
 967:                      "drug is for or is); the graph holds trials, not indications")
 968:     words = [w for w in words if w != "nsclc"]
 969: 
 970:     # letters spelled out one by one ("N O T") hide a word from every check
 971:     run = 0
 972:     for t in clause + [("P", "")]:
 973:         if t[0] == "W" and len(t[1]) == 1 and t[1] not in ("a", "i"):
 974:             run += 1
 975:             continue
 976:         if run >= 2:
 977:             flag("unreadable", "spelled-out letters", "letters written one by one cannot "
 978:                                                       "be read as a word and checked")
 979:         run = 0
 980: 
 981:     # a word naming a kind belongs to the entity just before it ("EGFR is a drug"),
 982:     # or, with none before, to an entity of that kind in the clause
 983:     for k, t in enumerate(clause):
 984:         if t[0] != "W" or t[1] not in KIND_WORDS or KIND_WORDS[t[1]] == "trials":
 985:             continue
 986:         kind = KIND_WORDS[t[1]]
 987:         prev_e = [ents[x[1]] for x in clause[:k] if x[0] == "ENT"]
 988:         ok = prev_e[-1]["type"] == kind if prev_e else kind in present
 989:         if not ok:
 990:             flag("wording", t[1], "calls something a drug or company that the evidence "
 991:                                   "does not")
 992: 
 993:     seen = set()
 994:     for w in words:
 995:         if w in seen:
 996:             continue
 997:         seen.add(w)
 998:         for vocab, kind, why in (
 999:                 (NEGATION, "negation", "the graph stores only relations that exist; it "
1000:                                        "cannot confirm an absence or a failure"),
1001:                 (COMPARISON, "comparison", "a ranking or comparison is not a relation "
1002:                                            "the graph holds"),
1003:                 (HEDGE, "speculation", "a guess about what may be true is not in the "
1004:                                        "evidence"),
1005:                 (DEVELOP, "development_claim", "the graph records trial sponsorship, "
1006:                                                "which is not evidence of developing, "
1007:                                                "owning or marketing a drug")):
1008:             if w in vocab or (kind == "negation" and (w.endswith("n't") or
1009:                                                        w.startswith("non_"))):
1010:                 flag(kind, w, why)
1011:                 break
1012:         else:
1013:             if w in KIND_WORDS:
1014:                 kind = KIND_WORDS[w]
1015:                 ok = about_trials if kind == "trials" else True   # checked above by owner
1016:                 if not ok:
1017:                     flag("wording", w, "calls something a drug, company or trial that "
1018:                                        "the evidence does not")
1019:                 continue
1020:             if w in RELATION or w in FUNCTION or w in BE or len(w) < 2:
1021:                 continue
1022:             if w in sup_tokens or STEM_SUPPORT.get(w) in sup_tokens:
1023:                 continue
1024:             flag("wording", w, "this word makes a claim no evidence statement about "
1025:                                "these entities supports")

FILE src/app.py
   1: """A local page for trying the pipeline by hand.
   2: 
   3:     python src/app.py            then open http://localhost:8765
   4: 
   5: Three boxes: type an intervention string the way a registry would write it and see
   6: how the resolver reads it, or ask a question and see the grounded answer with its
   7: evidence, or paste an answer (say, one a language model wrote) and see what the
   8: groundedness check flags against the evidence for that question. All three call
   9: the functions the pipeline uses (resolve_drug_string, OncoKG.answer,
  10: OncoKG.check_groundedness) on the built artefacts, so what the page shows is what the
  11: pipeline would do. Nothing is written; the language model is never called.
  12: """
  13: from __future__ import annotations
  14: 
  15: import json
  16: import sqlite3
  17: import sys
  18: import threading
  19: from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
  20: from pathlib import Path
  21: 
  22: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
  23: import config as C
  24: import normalize as N
  25: from query import OncoKG
  26: 
  27: PAGE = C.WEB / "try.html"
  28: PORT = 8765
  29: 
  30: 
  31: class Engine:
  32:     def __init__(self):
  33:         self.kg = OncoKG()
  34:         # A browser opens spare connections it may never use, which would stall a
  35:         # single-threaded server; requests are threaded, the engine is not.
  36:         self.kg.conn.close()
  37:         self.kg.conn = sqlite3.connect(C.PROCESSED / "oncokg.sqlite", check_same_thread=False)
  38:         self.kg.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
  39:         self.lock = threading.Lock()
  40:         self.dic = self.kg.dic
  41:         self.index_keys = self.kg.index_keys
  42:         target_rows, _, _ = N._load("targets")
  43:         self.target_keys = N.target_guard_keys(target_rows, self.dic)
  44: 
  45:     def resolve(self, text: str) -> dict:
  46:         out = N.resolve_drug_string(text, self.dic, self.index_keys, self.target_keys)
  47:         for c in out["components"]:
  48:             d = self.dic["drugs"].get(c.get("drug_id") or "")
  49:             if d:
  50:                 c["drug"] = {k: d.get(k) for k in
  51:                              ("name", "drug_type", "max_phase_overall", "max_phase_in_nsclc")}
  52:                 c["drug"]["mechanisms"] = [m.get("mechanismOfAction") if isinstance(m, dict) else m
  53:                                            for m in (d.get("mechanisms") or [])][:3]
  54:                 c["drug"]["trials"] = self.kg.conn.execute(
  55:                     "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
  56:                     (d["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]
  57:                 par = self.dic["drugs"].get(d.get("parent_molecule") or "")
  58:                 if par:
  59:                     c["drug"]["parent"] = {"name": par["name"], "trials": self.kg.conn.execute(
  60:                         "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE rel='EVALUATES' AND dst=? AND retired_at IS NULL",
  61:                         (par["drug_id"],)).fetchone()[0]}
  62:             if c.get("candidates"):
  63:                 c["candidate_names"] = [
  64:                     (self.dic["drugs"].get(x) or {}).get("name", x) for x in c["candidates"]]
  65:         return out
  66: 
  67:     def ask(self, question: str) -> dict:
  68:         return self.kg.answer(question, use_llm=False).to_dict()
  69: 
  70:     def check(self, question: str, answer: str) -> dict:
  71:         """Check an answer someone else wrote against the evidence for the question."""
  72:         kg = self.kg
  73:         entities = kg.link_entities(question)
  74:         intent = kg.classify(question, entities)
  75:         evidence = kg.retrieve(intent, entities)
  76:         checks = kg.check_groundedness(answer, evidence, entities, generated=True,
  77:                                        vocabulary=set(self.dic["index"]),
  78:                                        linker=kg.link_entities)
  79:         return {"intent": intent, "entities": entities, "checks": checks,
  80:                 "evidence": [{"statement": e.statement, "source_record": e.source_record,
  81:                               "source": e.source, "url": e.url} for e in evidence[:8]],
  82:                 "evidence_count": len(evidence)}
  83: 
  84: 
  85: def make_handler(engine: Engine):
  86:     class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
  87:         def _send(self, code: int, body: bytes, ctype: str):
  88:             self.send_response(code)
  89:             self.send_header("Content-Type", ctype)
  90:             self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
  91:             self.end_headers()
  92:             self.wfile.write(body)
  93: 
  94:         def do_GET(self):
  95:             if self.path in ("/", "/index.html"):
  96:                 self._send(200, PAGE.read_bytes(), "text/html; charset=utf-8")
  97:             else:
  98:                 self._send(404, b"not found", "text/plain")
  99: 
 100:         def do_POST(self):
 101:             try:
 102:                 n = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length") or 0)
 103:                 body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(min(n, 20000)) or b"{}")
 104:                 text = str(body.get("text") or "").strip()[:500]
 105:                 if not text:
 106:                     raise ValueError("empty input")
 107:                 if self.path not in ("/api/resolve", "/api/ask", "/api/check"):
 108:                     return self._send(404, b"{}", "application/json")
 109:                 with engine.lock:
 110:                     if self.path == "/api/resolve":
 111:                         res = engine.resolve(text)
 112:                     elif self.path == "/api/ask":
 113:                         res = engine.ask(text)
 114:                     else:
 115:                         answer = str(body.get("answer") or "").strip()[:4000]
 116:                         if not answer:
 117:                             raise ValueError("empty answer")
 118:                         res = engine.check(text, answer)
 119:                 self._send(200, json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
 120:                            "application/json; charset=utf-8")
 121:             except Exception as e:  # show the reason on the page, not a dead request
 122:                 self._send(400, json.dumps({"error": str(e)}, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8"),
 123:                            "application/json; charset=utf-8")
 124: 
 125:         def log_message(self, *a):
 126:             pass
 127:     return Handler
 128: 
 129: 
 130: def main() -> None:
 131:     port = int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else PORT
 132:     engine = Engine()
 133:     print(f"OncoKG try-it page: http://localhost:{port}", flush=True)
 134:     ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", port), make_handler(engine)).serve_forever()
 135: 
 136: 
 137: if __name__ == "__main__":
 138:     main()

FILE src/query.py
 435:             "numbers or claims that are not in it. Cite the record id in square "
 436:             "brackets after each claim. If the evidence does not answer the "
 437:             "question, say so plainly.\n\n"
 438:             f"Question: {question}\n\nEvidence:\n{block}\n")
 439:         client = anthropic.Anthropic()
 440:         msg = client.messages.create(
 441:             model="claude-sonnet-4-5", max_tokens=700,
 442:             messages=[{"role": "user", "content": prompt}])
 443:         return msg.content[0].text, "language model, constrained to retrieved evidence"
 444: 
 445:     # -- answer quality ---------------------------------------------------- #
 446:     @staticmethod
 447:     def check_groundedness(text: str, evidence: list[Evidence],
 448:                            entities: list[dict], generated: bool = True,
 449:                            vocabulary: set | None = None, linker=None) -> dict:
 450:         """Report what in a *generated* answer the retrieved evidence does not support.
 451: 
 452:         Two things this check is not, both found by an adversarial review and
 453:         written down here rather than papered over:
 454: 
 455:         1. It is meaningless on the template answer. The template is built by
 456:            concatenating the evidence statements, so checking it against those
 457:            same statements is circular -- it cannot fail, and reporting
 458:            `grounded=True` for it would be a test that always passes. When the
 459:            answer was rendered rather than generated, the check reports
 460:            `applicable=False` and asserts nothing.
 461:         2. It is a reader of word order, not of meaning. Without a `linker` it
 462:            only checks that capitalised entities, drug-shaped names and numbers
 463:            appear somewhere in the retrieved set. With one (the query layer's
 464:            entity linker) it also reads each clause into subject, relation and
 465:            object (claims.check_claims) and reports relations, values and words
 466:            the evidence does not give for those entities. Sentence shapes it was
 467:            not written for can still be misread; claims.py and README known
 468:            defect 13 list what is known to get through.
 469:         """
 470:         if not generated:
 471:             return {
 472:                 "grounded": None,
 473:                 "applicable": False,
 474:                 "unsupported_claims": [],
 475:                 "evidence_count": len(evidence),
 476:                 "method": "not applicable",
 477:                 "why": ("the answer was rendered directly from the retrieved "
 478:                         "evidence, so checking it against that evidence is "
 479:                         "circular and would always pass"),
 480:             }
 481: 
 482:         if linker is not None:
 483:             # a citation whose every id is a record of this evidence is a citation,
 484:             # not a claim; anything else in brackets stays and is checked
 485:             from claims import _strip_citations
 486:             known = {x.strip().lower() for e in evidence
 487:                      for part in (e.source_record or "", e.edge_id or "", e.statement or "")
 488:                      for x in re.split(r"[,;\s|]+", part) if x.strip()}
 489:             text = _strip_citations(text, known)
 490: 
 491:         haystack = " ".join(
 492:             [e.statement for e in evidence]
 493:             + [str(v) for e in evidence for v in e.detail.values()]
 494:             + [e.source_record or "" for e in evidence]
 495:             + [en["label"] for en in entities]
 496:         ).lower()
 497:         # Words are tokenised so "47" is not satisfied by NCT02303977. Numbers
 498:         # are tokenised separately and whole, decimals included: splitting on
 499:         # non-alphanumerics turned evidence "47.5" into the tokens "47" and
 500:         # "5", so an invented "47" passed and the exact "47.5" was rejected.
 501:         haystack_tokens = set(re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", haystack))
 502:         haystack_numbers = {n.replace(",", "") for n in RE_NUMBER.findall(haystack)}
 503: 
 504:         unsupported: list[dict] = []
 505: 
 506:         def alias_in_evidence(word: str) -> bool:
 507:             if linker is None:
 508:                 return False
 509:             return any(e.get("type") in ("Drug", "Target", "Company")
 510:                        and e["label"].lower() in haystack for e in linker(word))
 511: 
 512:         # "phase 3" is stated as PHASE3 in the evidence, so a phase number is
 513:         # matched against that form, not against the bare figures
 514:         haystack_flat = re.sub(r"[\s_\-]+", "", haystack)
 515:         phase_nums = {n for m in re.finditer(r"\bphase\s*(\d)(?:\s*/\s*(\d))?\b", text, re.I)
 516:                       for n in m.groups() if n}
 517:         # digits inside a linked name ("AZD 9291") are part of the name, not a figure
 518:         number_text = text
 519:         if linker is not None:
 520:             for _ in range(8):           # the linker reports each entity once
 521:                 before = number_text
 522:                 for e in linker(number_text):
 523:                     mt = e.get("matched_text") or ""
 524:                     if mt and e.get("type") != "Refused" and re.search(r"\d", mt):
 525:                         number_text = re.sub(r"[\W_]+".join(map(re.escape, mt.split())),
 526:                                              " ", number_text, flags=re.I)
 527:                 if number_text == before:
 528:                     break
 529:         for num in {n.rstrip(",") for n in RE_NUMBER.findall(number_text)}:
 530:             if num in phase_nums and (linker is not None or f"phase{num}" in haystack_flat):
 531:                 continue      # with a linker, claims.py checks the phase per entity
 532:             if num.replace(",", "") not in haystack_numbers:
 533:                 unsupported.append({"kind": "number", "value": num,
 534:                                     "why": "not present in the retrieved evidence"})
 535: 
 536:         # A figure written in words cannot be matched against the evidence, so
 537:         # it is reported as unverifiable rather than allowed through.
 538:         for word in set(RE_NUMBER_WORD.findall(text.lower())):
 539:             unsupported.append({"kind": "number_in_words", "value": word,
 540:                                 "why": "a figure written in words cannot be "
 541:                                        "checked against the evidence"})
 542: 
 543:         capitalised = set(re.findall(r"\b[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9\-]{3,}\b", text))
 544:         stop = {"Scope", "Organisations", "Trials", "Profile", "Findings",
 545:                 "Agents", "Reported", "This", "The", "None", "NSCLC", "What",
 546:                 "There", "These", "That", "Those", "According", "Sponsors",
 547:                 "They", "Them", "Their", "Its"}
 548:         for name in capitalised - stop:
 549:             if name.lower() not in haystack and not alias_in_evidence(name):
 550:                 unsupported.append({"kind": "entity", "value": name,
 551:                                     "why": "named in the answer but not in the evidence"})
 552: 
 553:         # Drug names are usually lowercase in prose ("xyzabumab cures cancer"
 554:         # passed the capitalised-only check). Anything shaped like an INN or a
 555:         # development code must appear in the evidence too.
 556:         for name in set(RE_DRUGLIKE.findall(text)):
 557:             if (name.lower() not in haystack_tokens and name.lower() not in haystack
 558:                     and not alias_in_evidence(name)):
 559:                 unsupported.append({"kind": "drug_like_name", "value": name,
 560:                                     "why": "looks like a drug name or code but "
 561:                                            "is not in the evidence"})
 562: 
 563:         # Any word the drug vocabulary knows ("aspirin") is a drug name,
 564:         # whatever its shape, and must be in the evidence.
 565:         if vocabulary:
 566:             for w in set(re.findall(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9\-]{3,}", text)):
 567:                 if (T.canonical_key(w) in vocabulary and w.lower() not in haystack
 568:                         and not alias_in_evidence(w)):
 569:                     unsupported.append({"kind": "drug_name", "value": w,
 570:                                         "why": "a known drug name not in the evidence"})
 571: 
 572:         # Figures in forms the number matcher does not read: scientific
 573:         # notation ("1e6") and Unicode fractions or superscripts ("⅔").
 574:         for odd in set(RE_ODD_NUMBER.findall(text)):
 575:             unsupported.append({"kind": "number_unreadable", "value": odd,
 576:                                 "why": "a figure in a form the check cannot match"})
 577: 
 578:         if linker is not None:
 579:             from claims import check_claims
 580:             unsupported.extend(check_claims(text, evidence, linker, entities))
 581: 
 582:         # one finding per value, first reason kept, in a stable order
 583:         seen_values: set[str] = set()
 584:         key = lambda u: (str(u["value"]), u.get("sentence", ""))
 585:         unsupported = [u for u in sorted(unsupported, key=lambda u: (*key(u), u["kind"]))
 586:                        if not (key(u) in seen_values or seen_values.add(key(u)))]
 587: 
 588:         return {
 589:             "grounded": not unsupported,
 590:             "applicable": True,
 620:         "Which drugs act on ALK?",
 621:         "What is AstraZeneca running in NSCLC?",
 622:         "What is the capital of France?",
 623:     ]
 624:     kg = OncoKG()
 625:     out = []
 626:     for q in questions:
 627:         a = kg.answer(q)
 628:         out.append(a.to_dict())
 629:         print("=" * 72)
 630:         print("Q:", q)
 631:         print(f"   intent={a.intent}  entities="
 632:               f"{[(e['type'], e['label']) for e in a.entities]}")
 633:         print(f"   generated_by={a.generated_by}")
 634:         print(a.text)
 635:         g = a.checks
 636:         print(f"   grounded={g['grounded']}  unsupported={g['unsupported_claims']}")
 637:     (C.REPORTS / "query_examples.json").write_text(
 638:         json.dumps(out, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
 639:     print("\n->", (C.REPORTS / "query_examples.json").relative_to(C.ROOT))

 643:     main()

FILE README.md
 495: 11. **The registry-synonym rule errs toward refusal.** It removes some correct
 496:     names along with the contaminated ones: misspellings it cannot tell from a
 497:     different ingredient (`terelizumab` for tislelizumab, `slulimumab` for
 498:     serplulimab), code names whose molecule record does not list them
 499:     (`limertinib` for ASK-120067, `pankomab-gex` for gatipotuzumab, four
 500:     variants under disitamab vedotin), and short abbreviations that are
 501:     ambiguous across the vocabulary (`epi`, `hr3`, `il-2`). Each removal is a
 502:     finding in the quality report with its reason, so they can be reviewed.
 503: 12. **The corporate map is a dated assertion.** Its 56 parent keys and 8
 504:     same-entity keys were checked for the
 505:     obvious changes (spin-offs, renames, the two Mercks), but continued legal
 506:     existence of every subsidiary is unverified, and nothing re-checks the map
 507:     when ownership changes.
 508: 13. **The claim check reads word order, not meaning.** Generated answers are read
 509:     clause by clause into subject, relation and object (`src/claims.py`, rules in
 510:     its docstring) and compared with the retrieved evidence for those entities. It
 511:     is a set of patterns, so it is only as good as the sentence shapes it was
 512:     written for. Four adversarial rounds found 33, about 40, about 25 and then
 513:     about 20 shapes that got through (reversed nouns, clefts, a second object after "and
 514:     on", pronouns read too early, plural pronouns, a count borrowed from a phase or
 515:     shared by two drugs, `agonist` inside `antagonist`, headings, bracketed text,
 516:     refused product names, words like "clinical trials" or "drug" treated as
 517:     grammar, "approved in NSCLC", "the following safe drugs", a phase or status
 518:     shared by two trials, letters spelled out one by one). Each is now a regression
 519:     test, but a shape
 520:     nobody has tried yet can still be misread. Known to get through: a clause whose
 521:     words, entities and figures all come from one evidence statement about those
 522:     entities but are arranged into a different claim; a drug name outside the
 523:     vocabulary that does not look like an INN; odd but evidence-consistent wording
 524:     ("osimertinib is the inhibition of EGFR"). Known to be over-strict: a true fact
 525:     the graph does not hold, a paraphrase it has no word for ("supports trials"), a
 526:     sentence about the evidence itself that uses a negation or "develops", and a
 527:     refusal naming something outside the graph ("the capital of France"). Three
 528:     narrow exceptions are deliberate: a line copied from an evidence statement
 529:     passes (its heading is still checked), a clause with no entity that only talks
 530:     about the graph passes, and a bracket is dropped as a citation only when every
 531:     id in it is a record of the retrieved evidence.
 532: 14. **Provenance does not record the code version.** Snapshots carry their
 533:     fetch time, but the graph does not store which revision of the rules built
 534:     it, so a change that arrives with a new snapshot and a code change together
 535:     cannot be separated from the database alone; only git history can.
 536: 8. **The query layer answers the nearest question it can.** "Is pembrolizumab
 537:    safe in patients with autoimmune disease?" returns a drug profile rather than
 538:    refusing. It refuses only when no entity links at all.
 539: 9. **`MEDI4736` stays ambiguous** although it is unambiguously durvalumab,
 540:    because the contaminated synonym blocks the preferred-name rule.
 541: 10. **A two-word personal name is indistinguishable from a short company name.**
 542:     `Xiuning Le` is a person and `Shanghai Henlius` is a company; only an
 543:     explicit credential (`MD`, `PhD`) is used to separate them, so the 36
 544:     `Investigator` nodes are the credentialed ones and the rest stay `Company`.
 545:     An earlier shape-based guess typed Accuray and Daiichi Sankyo as people.
 546: 
 547: ---

343: def _check_unit(unit, statements, linker, st, flag, lenient=False, joined=False):
344:     toks, ents = _tokenise(unit, linker)
345:     for clause in _clauses(toks):
346:         # pronouns take the subject as it stands at this clause, not at the unit start;
347:         # one after a relation verb is that relation's object ("targets EGFR and
348:         # inhibits it"); a plural one stands for every member of the subject group
349:         plural = None
350:         for k, t in enumerate(clause):
351:             if t[0] != "ENT" or ents[t[1]]["type"] != "Pronoun":
352:                 continue
353:             p = ents[t[1]]
354:             want = p["want"]
355:             back = [x for x in clause[max(0, k - 2):k] if x[0] == "W"]
356:             if want is None and back and back[-1][1] in set(RELATION) | {"on", "of"}:
357:                 verb = next((x[1] for x in reversed(back) if x[1] in RELATION), None)
358:                 if verb:
359:                     want = ROLES[RELATION[verb][0]][1]
360:             if want is None:
361:                 # "they" (or an "it" that could be either) after a clause naming several
362:                 # entities of one kind stands for all of them: the subject group, or
363:                 # else the largest set of one kind ("ALK is targeted by A and B")
364:                 kinds_last: dict[str, list] = {}
365:                 for e in st.last:
366:                     kinds_last.setdefault(e.get("type"), []).append(e)
367:                 biggest = max(kinds_last.values(), key=len, default=[])
368:                 members = (list(st.group) if len(st.group) > 1 else
369:                            biggest if p["label"] in PLURAL and len(biggest) > 1 else [])
370:             else:
371:                 members = ([e for e in st.last if e.get("type") == want]
372:                            if p["label"] in PLURAL else [])
373:             if len(members) > 1:
374:                 plural = (t[1], members)
375:                 ents[t[1]] = dict(members[0])
376:                 continue
377:             ante = st.by_type.get(want) if want else st.subject
378:             ents[t[1]] = (dict(ante, _pron=True) if ante
379:                           else {"type": "Missing", "label": p["label"]})
380:         pred = next((i for i, t in enumerate(clause)
381:                      if t[0] == "W" and t[1] in PREDICATE), len(clause))
382:         elided = pred < len(clause) and not any(t[0] == "ENT" for t in clause[:pred])
383:         if plural:
384:             idx, members = plural
385:             for m in members:                 # "they are approved": each of them
386:                 ents[idx] = dict(m)
387:                 _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)
388:             if idx in [t[1] for t in clause[:pred] if t[0] == "ENT"]:
389:                 st.restore(members, members)  # "they" stays the group for what follows
390:             else:
391:                 names = {m["label"] for m in members}
392:                 st.last = [e for e in st.last if e.get("label") not in names] + members
393:         elif elided and len(st.group) > 1 and not lenient:
394:             members, last = list(st.group), list(st.last)
395:             for m in members:                 # "LORLATINIB and PLB1003 ... and are approved"
396:                 st.subject = m
397:                 _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)
398:             st.restore(members, last)
399:         else:
400:             _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient, joined)

403: def _check_clause(clause, ents, statements, st, flag, lenient=False, joined=False):
404:     if True:
405:         c_ents = [ents[t[1]] for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"]
406:         words = [t[1] for t in clause if t[0] == "W"]
407:         has_num = any(t[0] == "N" for t in clause)
408: 
409:         refused = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] == "Refused"]
410:         for e in refused:
411:             flag("distinct_product", e["label"], "names a distinct product (biosimilar, "
412:                  "other formulation, tracer) the graph does not hold")
413:         c_ents = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] != "Refused"]
414: 
415:         gone = [e for e in c_ents if e["type"] == "Missing"]
416:         if len(gone) == len(c_ents) and not has_num and all(
417:                 w in META_OK or w.endswith("n't") or w in FUNCTION
418:                 or (w in KIND_WORDS and META_ANCHOR & set(words)) for w in words) and (
419:                 not any(w in KIND_WORDS or w in FUNCTION for w in words if w not in META_OK)
420:                 or META_ANCHOR & set(words)):
421:             return                            # "this graph cannot answer that"
422:         if gone:
423:             flag("no_antecedent", gone[0]["label"],
424:                  "a pronoun with nothing before it to refer to")
425:             return
426: 
427:         pred = next((i for i, t in enumerate(clause)
428:                      if t[0] == "W" and t[1] in PREDICATE), len(clause))
429:         implicit = None
430:         if pred < len(clause) and not lenient and not any(
431:                 t[0] == "ENT" and ents[t[1]]["type"] != "Refused" for t in clause[:pred]):
432:             implicit = st.subject             # "... and is phase 3"
433:         subjects = c_ents + ([implicit] if implicit and implicit not in c_ents else [])
434:         if not subjects:
435:             content = [w for w in words if w not in FUNCTION and w not in BE]
436:             if content or has_num:
437:                 flag("no_subject", " ".join(content[:6]) or "figure",
438:                      "this clause names no entity the evidence can be checked against")
439:             return
440:         labels = [e["label"].lower() for e in subjects]
441:         support = [(r, s) for r, s in statements if all(_has(l, s) for l in labels)]
442:         covered = _check_relations(clause, ents, implicit, statements, flag, lenient)
443:         left_e = {ents[t[1]]["label"].lower() for t in clause[:pred] if t[0] == "ENT"} or (
444:             {implicit["label"].lower()} if implicit else set())
445:         right_e = {ents[t[1]]["label"].lower() for t in clause[pred:] if t[0] == "ENT"}
740:                  f"this relation runs from a {subj_t.lower()} to a {obj_t.lower()}; "
741:                  "the sentence has it the other way or between the wrong kinds")
742:             continue
743:         for x in left:
744:             for y in right:
745:                 hits = [s for r, s in statements if r == rel and _has(x["label"], s)
746:                         and _has(y["label"], s)]
747:                 if not hits:
748:                     flag("relation", f"{x['label']} — {t[1]} — {y['label']}",
749:                          "no retrieved edge links these two entities this way")
750:                 elif action and not any(re.search(rf"\b{a}\b", s)
751:                                         for s in hits for a in ACTION_WORDS[action]):
752:                     flag("action", f"{x['label']} {t[1]} {y['label']}",
753:                          "the evidence gives a different or no action type")
754:     return covered if any_rel else None

756: 
757: def _owner(clause, ents, i, prefer, kinds=("Drug", "Target", "Company", "Trial")):
758:     """The entity a value at position i belongs to: nearest in `prefer` direction first,
759:     among the kinds of entity that can carry that value."""
760:     order = [range(i + 1, len(clause)), range(i - 1, -1, -1)]
761:     if prefer == "before":
762:         order.reverse()
763:     for rng in order:
764:         for j in rng:
765:             if clause[j][0] == "ENT" and ents[clause[j][1]]["type"] in kinds:
766:                 return ents[clause[j][1]]
767:     return None
768: 
769: 
770: def _check_attributes(clause, ents, subjects, support, statements, flag):
771:     subject = subjects[0]
772:     present = {e["type"] for e in subjects} | {
773:         ents[t[1]]["type"] for t in clause if t[0] == "ENT"}
774:     has_company = "Company" in present
775:     companies = [e for e in subjects if e["type"] == "Company"]
776: 
777:     def scoped(i, prefer, count=False, kinds=("Drug", "Target", "Company", "Trial")):
778:         """Evidence naming the clause subject, any company the clause is about (said or
779:         carried over) and the value's own entity. A count of trials in a sponsorship
780:         statement belongs to the sponsor-drug pair, so it does not count for a clause
781:         that names no company."""
782:         own = _owner(clause, ents, i, prefer, kinds) or subject
783:         # a trial's phase is its own; any other value is about the clause's subject too
784:         names = ({own["label"].lower()} if own["type"] == "Trial" else
785:                  {own["label"].lower(), subject["label"].lower()} | {
786:                      c["label"].lower() for c in companies})
787:         rows = [s for r, s in statements if all(_has(n, s) for n in names)
788:                 and not (count and r == "SPONSORS_TRIAL_OF" and not has_company)]
789:         return own, rows
790: 
791:     consumed: set[int] = set()
792:     sup_tokens: set[str] = set()
793:     for _, s in support:
794:         sup_tokens |= set(re.findall(r"[a-z]+(?:_[a-z]+)*", s))
795:     sup_flat = " " + " ".join(_flat(s) for _, s in support) + " "
796:     # whether the clause is about trials (and NSCLC trials) is read from the statements
